        Idea:
Feature: Right-click context menu for selected gallery/timeline items with Create Shot and Generate Video actions. When items are selected (in gallery or timeline), right-clicking should show a context menu with: (1) 'Create Shot' — creates a new shot containing the selected items (images/videos). Should work for single or multiple selected items. (2) 'Generate Video' — creates a new shot with the selected items, then opens the video generation modal (the one that appears in shot view) pre-populated with that newly created shot. Both actions should work regardless of whether items were selected in the gallery, timeline, or across both. The context menu should appear on right-click on any selected item. Also need to ensure there are good paths/flows for creating shots from selected items via buttons (not just right-click) and for generating videos from those shots.

User notes and answers:
- User answers: Q1: Create shot then open the same VideoGenerationModal used in the shot list view on Video Travel Tool page (triggered by video icon button). Just create the shot and open that modal. Q2: Context menu should NOT show for non-media clips (no text, effects, audio). Q3: Use existing auto-naming convention — the app already auto-names shots (e.g. 'Shot X') when adding from gallery lightbox. Use that same pattern. Q4: Items should be POSITIONED — ordered based on their order on the timeline, or if from gallery, the order they were selected in. Q5: Skip floating action bar — just do context menu for now, figure out what makes sense for buttons later.

  "meta_commentary": "Key execution guidance:\n\n**T1 is the critical foundation** \u2014 get the sequential insertion right. The temptation to use `Promise.all` for speed is strong but will produce random ordering because each `add_generation_to_shot` RPC auto-calculates position from existing entries. A simple for-of loop with await is correct.\n\n**T2 is a quick verification** that unblocks T4 and T5. The exploration suggests VideoGenerationModal only needs ProjectContext (app-wide) and a Shot prop, but verify by tracing `useVideoGenerationModalController` imports. If it needs `useShotNavigation` or `useShotSettings` from ShotsContext, those are also available app-wide via ShotsProvider in AppProviders.\n\n**T3 is straightforward UI** \u2014 mirror ClipAction.tsx's portal pattern exactly. Don't use shadcn ContextMenu primitive \u2014 the plan specifies the manual portal approach for consistency with the existing timeline menu.\n\n**T4 and T5 are independent and can be developed in parallel** (they share T1-T3 as dependencies but don't depend on each other). The main complexity in T4 is threading `onContextMenu` through 4 component layers (GenerationsPaneGallery \u2192 MediaGallery \u2192 MediaGalleryGrid \u2192 MediaGalleryItem). In T5, the complexity is resolving clip IDs to TimelineActions for sorting \u2014 search `rows[i].actions.find(a => a.id === clipId)` to get both the action and trackIndex.\n\n**ShotsContext availability for T5**: `useShotCreation` needs ShotsContext. Check if TimelineEditor or its parents already provide this. If not, the hook may need to be called higher up and passed down as a callback prop.\n\n**The two open plan questions**: (1) Two-step approach (create empty shot + add images) is fine \u2014 atomicity isn't critical here since failure mid-way leaves a partially-populated shot which is still valid. (2) For loading feedback, close the context menu immediately and show `isCreating` state \u2014 the shot list will update via React Query invalidation when done.",

- [DEBT] properties-panel-scope: crop and transition controls are not included in the bulk panel, narrowing the original idea's scope. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] registry-patch-testing: patchregistry() lacks automated regression tests — the duplicate-id bug's most likely reproduction path is covered only by pure-function tests on the assembler it delegates to, plus manual smoke testing. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: checked the affected edge-agent surface around `llm/messages.ts`. the `isrecord` extraction is broader than the three files listed in step 6 because `messages.ts` is a shared utility module for `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`; the plan still scopes the edit as if only the local definition sites matter. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] tool-error-propagation: a blanket catch in executetool would silently downgrade db/persistence failures into ordinary tool results, changing the session error/http 500 behavior. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-accessibility: replacing arrow buttons with drag-only handle removes keyboard-accessible reorder; plan only specifies pointersensor. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-testing: no automated tests specified for same-kind boundary rules or save/load persistence of reordered tracks. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] vlm-cold-gpu-diagnostics: the cold-gpu vlm import crash investigation is deferred — the plan ships the zombie fix without root-causing the specific import failure. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] workspace-git-hygiene: step 2 discards unstaged edits in 6 files via git checkout head without first confirming the revert was unintentional. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

'cursor-wait opacity-60'\n+          : destructive\n+            ? 'hover:bg-destructive hover:text-destructive-foreground'\n+            : 'hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground',\n+      )}\n+      onClick={onClick}\n+      disabled={disabled}\n+    >\n+      {icon}\n+      {label}\n+    </button>\n+  );\n+}\n+\n+export function SelectionContextMenu({\n+  position,\n+  onClose,\n+  onCreateShot,\n+  onGenerateVideo,\n+  isCreating = false,\n+}: SelectionContextMenuProps) {\n+  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n+\n+  useEffect(() => {\n+    if (!position) {\n+      return undefined;\n+    }\n+\n+    const handleClickAway = (event: MouseEvent) => {\n+      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {\n+        onClose();\n+      }\n+    };\n+    const handleEscape = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {\n+      if (event.key === 'Escape') {\n+        onClose();\n+      }\n+    };\n+\n+    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickAway);\n+    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);\n+    return () => {\n+      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickAway);\n+      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);\n+    };\n+  }, [position, onClose]);\n+\n+  if (!position) {\n+    return null;\n+  }\n+\n+  const handleAction = (action: () => void | Promise<void>) => {\n+    onClose();\n+    void action();\n+  };\n+\n+  return createPortal(\n+    <div\n+      ref={menuRef}\n+      className=\"fixed z-50 min-w-[12rem] overflow-hidden rounded-md border bg-popover p-1 text-popover-foreground shadow-md animate-in fade-in-0 zoom-in-95\"\n+      style={{ left: position.x, top: position.y }}\n+    >\n+      <MenuItem\n+        icon={<FolderPlus className=\"h-4 w-4\" />}\n+        label=\"Create Shot\"\n+        onClick={() => handleAction(onCreateShot)}\n+        disabled={isCreating}\n+      />\n+      <MenuItem\n+        icon={<Clapperboard className=\"h-4 w-4\" />}\n+        label=\"Generate Video\"\n+        onClick={() => handleAction(onGenerateVideo)}\n+        disabled={isCreating}\n+      />\n+    </div>,\n+    document.body,\n+  );\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Project directory:

        Idea:
Feature: Right-click context menu for selected gallery/timeline items with Create Shot and Generate Video actions. When items are selected (in gallery or timeline), right-clicking should show a context menu with: (1) 'Create Shot' — creates a new shot containing the selected items (images/videos). Should work for single or multiple selected items. (2) 'Generate Video' — creates a new shot with the selected items, then opens the video generation modal (the one that appears in shot view) pre-populated with that newly created shot. Both actions should work regardless of whether items were selected in the gallery, timeline, or across both. The context menu should appear on right-click on any selected item. Also need to ensure there are good paths/flows for creating shots from selected items via buttons (not just right-click) and for generating videos from those shots.

User notes and answers:
- User answers: Q1: Create shot then open the same VideoGenerationModal used in the shot list view on Video Travel Tool page (triggered by video icon button). Just create the shot and open that modal. Q2: Context menu should NOT show for non-media clips (no text, effects, audio). Q3: Use existing auto-naming convention — the app already auto-names shots (e.g. 'Shot X') when adding from gallery lightbox. Use that same pattern. Q4: Items should be POSITIONED — ordered based on their order on the timeline, or if from gallery, the order they were selected in. Q5: Skip floating action bar — just do context menu for now, figure out what makes sense for buttons later.

        Batch framing:
        - Execute batch 2 of 3.
        - Actionable task IDs for this batch: ['T4', 'T5']
        - Already completed task IDs available as dependency context: ['T1', 'T2', 'T3']

- [DEBT] edit-mode-persistence: no read-side normalization test for legacy 'enhance' values (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation grep for 'enhance' will false-fail due to video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: grep scope too broad — hits video submode code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] edit-mode-validation: validation scope reaches unrelated video code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] effect-layer-overlap-enforcement: the plan relies on same-track overlap being forbidden but does not explicitly specify the enforcement call (resolveoverlaps or range rejection) in the drop/create path. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them: find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the current callers of `isrecord` from `llm/messages.ts`. `index.ts` uses it to validate `rawsession`, `db.ts` uses it on db rows, `tool-calls.ts` uses it on parsed tool-call payloads and nested `function` objects, and `selectedclips.ts` uses it while normalizing attachments, so preserving that import contract matters to four different call paths. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] legacy-effect-lifecycle: removing customeffecteditor makes effects-table entries read-only — users can no longer edit legacy custom effects. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] properties-panel-scope: crop and transition controls are not included in the bulk panel, narrowing the original idea's scope. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] registry-patch-testing: patchregistry() lacks automated regression tests — the duplicate-id bug's most likely reproduction path is covered only by pure-function tests on the assembler it delegates to, plus manual smoke testing. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: checked the affected edge-agent surface around `llm/messages.ts`. the `isrecord` extraction is broader than the three files listed in step 6 because `messages.ts` is a shared utility module for `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`; the plan still scopes the edit as if only the local definition sites matter. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] tool-error-propagation: a blanket catch in executetool would silently downgrade db/persistence failures into ordinary tool results, changing the session error/http 500 behavior. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-accessibility: replacing arrow buttons with drag-only handle removes keyboard-accessible reorder; plan only specifies pointersensor. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-testing: no automated tests specified for same-kind boundary rules or save/load persistence of reordered tracks. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] vlm-cold-gpu-diagnostics: the cold-gpu vlm import crash investigation is deferred — the plan ships the zombie fix without root-causing the specific import failure. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] workspace-git-hygiene: step 2 discards unstaged edits in 6 files via git checkout head without first confirming the revert was unintentional. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Feature: Right-click context menu for selected gallery/timeline items with Create Shot and Generate Video actions. When items are selected (in gallery or timeline), right-clicking should show a context menu with: (1) 'Create Shot' — creates a new shot containing the selected items (images/videos). Should work for single or multiple selected items. (2) 'Generate Video' — creates a new shot with the selected items, then opens the video generation modal (the one that appears in shot view) pre-populated with that newly created shot. Both actions should work regardless of whether items were selected in the gallery, timeline, or across both. The context menu should appear on right-click on any selected item. Also need to ensure there are good paths/flows for creating shots from selected items via buttons (not just right-click) and for generating videos from those shots.

User notes and answers:
- User answers: Q1: Create shot then open the same VideoGenerationModal used in the shot list view on Video Travel Tool page (triggered by video icon button). Just create the shot and open that modal. Q2: Context menu should NOT show for non-media clips (no text, effects, audio). Q3: Use existing auto-naming convention — the app already auto-names shots (e.g. 'Shot X') when adding from gallery lightbox. Use that same pattern. Q4: Items should be POSITIONED — ordered based on their order on the timeline, or if from gallery, the order they were selected in. Q5: Skip floating action bar — just do context menu for now, figure out what makes sense for buttons later.

        Batch framing:
        - Execute batch 3 of 3.
        - Actionable task IDs for this batch: ['T6']
        - Already completed task IDs available as dependency context: ['T1', 'T2', 'T3', 'T4', 'T5']

  "meta_commentary": "Key execution guidance:\n\n**T1 is the critical foundation** \u2014 get the sequential insertion right. The temptation to use `Promise.all` for speed is strong but will produce random ordering because each `add_generation_to_shot` RPC auto-calculates position from existing entries. The exploration suggests VideoGenerationModal only needs ProjectContext (app-wide) and a Shot prop, but verify by tracing `useVideoGenerationModalController` imports. If it needs `useShotNavigation` or `useShotSettings` from ShotsContext, those are also available app-wide via ShotsProvider in AppProviders.\n\n**T3 is straightforward UI** \u2014 mirror ClipAction.tsx's portal pattern exactly. Don't use shadcn ContextMenu primitive \u2014 the plan specifies the manual portal approach for consistency with the existing timeline menu.\n\n**T4 and T5 are independent and can be developed in parallel** (they share T1-T3 as dependencies but don't depend on each other). The main complexity in T4 is threading `onContextMenu` through 4 component layers (GenerationsPaneGallery \u2192 MediaGallery \u2192 MediaGalleryGrid \u2192 MediaGalleryItem). In T5, the complexity is resolving clip IDs to TimelineActions for sorting \u2014 search `rows[i].actions.find(a => a.id === clipId)` to get both the action and trackIndex.\n\n**ShotsContext availability for T5**: `useShotCreation` needs ShotsContext. Check if TimelineEditor or its parents already provide this. If not, the hook may need to be called higher up and passed down as a callback prop.\n\n**The two open plan questions**: (1) Two-step approach (create empty shot + add images) is fine \u2014 atomicity isn't critical here since failure mid-way leaves a partially-populated shot which is still valid. (2) For loading feedback, close the context menu immediately and show `isCreating` state \u2014 the shot list will update via React Query invalidation when done.",

        - If a test fails and you determine the failure is pre-existing (fails without your changes too), you MUST still re-run the FULL test suite with your changes applied. Pre-existing failures do not excuse you from verifying all other tests still pass. Never narrow to individual test functions and stop.
        - If this batch includes the final verification task, write a short script that reproduces the exact bug described in the task, run it to confirm the fix resolves it, then delete the script.
